### Bilateral studies guide different research questions

Bryant and colleagues' [2019]{.allow-number data-number-kind="year"} paper, *A Survey of Consumer Perceptions of [Plant-Based]{.keep-word} and Clean Meat in the USA, India, and China*, reports an Indian subgroup of [1024]{.claim data-claim="acquisition_in_d03_sample_n"} respondents. Its [English-language]{.keep-word} online panel is disproportionately urban, affluent and educated. The questionnaire concerns hypothetical purchases after product descriptions; it does not measure observed sales. The saved original supports the subgroup size and selection limits, but an exact fieldwork period was not located. [Bryant and colleagues, Participants and Limitations](<https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/sustainable-food-systems/articles/10.3389/fsufs.2019.00011/full>). This study suggests questions about which consumers are represented before any national inference is attempted.

Arora, Brent and Jaenicke's [2020]{.allow-number data-number-kind="year"} *Is India Ready for [Alt-Meat]{.keep-word}?* uses a [face-to-face]{.keep-word} [discrete-choice]{.keep-word} survey in Mumbai from December [2018]{.allow-number data-number-kind="year"}. The English/Hindi instrument compared conventional, [plant-based]{.keep-word} and [cell-based]{.keep-word} meat with chana. Recruitment near railway markets and in malls or supermarkets sought varied incomes; the analysis excludes incomplete interviews. The original methodology and instrument descriptions are available in the [publisher's full paper](<https://mdpi-res.com/d_attachment/sustainability/sustainability-12-04377/article_deploy/sustainability-12-04377.pdf>). Its local stated choices make the familiar meal alternative worth investigating, without identifying national buying behavior. No [willingness-to-pay]{.keep-word} coefficient or acceptance percentage from this paper enters the current numerical synthesis.

Ahsan, Uzair and Ali's *Attitudes and Perceptions Towards Cultured Meat Among General Population in Pakistan* used [social-media]{.keep-word} recruitment and an online questionnaire, with interviewer assistance where literacy prevented [self-completion]{.keep-word}. The original methods place fieldwork in January to March [2021]{.allow-number data-number-kind="year"}; its title page distinguishes online publication in [2021]{.allow-number data-number-kind="year"} from the [2022]{.allow-number data-number-kind="year"} journal issue. Ahsan, Uzair and Ali, Study Design and Results (publisher access unavailable in the recorded [direct-link]{.keep-word} audit; original bibliographic URL retained in the companion source CSV). A [research-browser]{.keep-word} reading of the original publisher PDF confirmed these descriptions, while the [direct-link]{.keep-word} audit remained blocked. Sampling limitations and inconsistent acceptance reporting prevent selecting a single percentage as Pakistan's position. The instrument still identifies safety, price and religious compatibility as questions for a better specified local study.

Dean and colleagues' multinational paper examines [plant-based]{.keep-word}, fungal and [cultured-muscle]{.keep-word} alternatives in separate models. Its pooled analysis does not supply a [Pakistan-specific]{.keep-word} coefficient or an estimate for [precision-fermented]{.keep-word} dairy. [Dean and colleagues, [product-specific]{.keep-word} models](<https://hau.repository.guildhe.ac.uk/id/eprint/17927/>). The related mycoprotein paper reports a Pakistan subgroup of [649]{.claim data-claim="acquisition_pk_d01_sample_n"} from [2018–2019]{.allow-number data-number-kind="year"} fieldwork. The subgroup is young and [nonprobability-based]{.keep-word}, and the paper retains inconsistent pooled totals. [Dean and colleagues, mycoprotein methods and country sample table](<https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC9416216/>). Its [data-request]{.keep-word} route is distinct from having respondent records suitable for reanalysis.

::: {data-package="P06"}

The Phase Two search is an [access-route]{.keep-word} inventory. Its source records identify publications, repositories and possible contacts, with a separate contextual register. Discovery of a DOI, repository page or [data-availability]{.keep-word} statement is not acquisition of respondent records. The reported search checks establish the organization of those routes, not eligibility for a pooled analysis.

The inventory contains [43]{.claim data-claim="package_p06_access_routes"} [access-route]{.keep-word} records and [13]{.claim data-claim="package_p06_contextual_routes"} contextual entries. These counts describe records, not independent datasets or respondents. Overlapping papers and routes remain separate from a count of acquired data.

The next requests concern Dean's Pakistan data, the India Mendeley route and the Bryant OSF materials. Each requires confirmation of study identity, version, variables, sample definition, reuse terms and whether the available files actually contain respondent observations. A route whose title resembles a known paper remains unresolved until those checks succeed.

The planned India request concerns the Mendeley listing with a reported sample of [257]{.claim data-claim="acquisition_in_d01_sample_n"}. The separate listing with [350]{.claim data-claim="acquisition_in_d02_sample_n"} is an additional discovery lead. Neither listing establishes an acquired or usable respondent dataset, and the larger listed sample does not replace the planned request.

Request drafts exist but remain unsent. The research should first determine what each file could answer: product understanding, stated choice at a specified price, predictors within the sampled group or observed repeat purchase. It should not request data under an adoption label when the instrument measured only hypothetical willingness.

:::

## Halal access depends on the named product and decision

::: {data-package="P09"}

The [cultivated-chicken]{.keep-word} paper asks what must be established before a specified product reaches Muslim consumers through the relevant religious, certification and [food-authorization]{.keep-word} processes. Its framework separates donor procurement, culture inputs, transformation, category and edibility, certification, and food authorization. These are analytical questions for organizing the evidence, not a claim that every authority reasons in the same sequence. [Cultivated Chicken and Halal Market Access](https://cultivated-chicken-halal.vercel.app/)

The International Islamic Fiqh Academy's [2025]{.allow-number data-number-kind="year"} resolution makes its [cultivated-meat]{.keep-word} position conditional on the donor, culture inputs and oversight. It excludes prohibited culture media and ingredients, requires supervision by a credible specialist authority, and assigns disclosure and verification duties. Its donor wording distinguishes a living lawful animal from cases requiring lawful slaughter; that wording needs interpretation within the full resolution. [IIFA [cultivated-meat]{.keep-word} resolution](<https://iifa-aifi.org/en/56085.html>). These conditions do not constitute certification of a particular manufacturer's product.

Singapore's Islamic Religious Council (MUIS) stated in [2024]{.allow-number data-number-kind="year"} that the cell source must be a halal animal, ingredients contributing to texture and composition must be halal, and the product must be clean and [non-toxic]{.keep-word}. [MUIS [cultivated-meat]{.keep-word} guidance](<https://www.muis.gov.sg/resources/media-releases/3-feb-24-fatwa-on-cultivated-meat/>). That guidance concerns cultivated meat under Singapore's religious framework. It does not grant Pakistani food authorization or determine consumer willingness.

A founder cell line means cells selected and banked to start later production. The original animal sample, that banked line and the later biomass can raise different questions. A [serum-free]{.keep-word} production entry does not by itself document all earlier banking stages. Washing, measured residues, biological growth and legal transformation are distinct claims. The paper leaves the evidential connections between those stages open where a product dossier is missing.

The source audit distinguishes admitted evidence, held material and sources not retrieved. Every category retains its [human-review]{.keep-word} status. Classical passages, modern institutional decisions, applicant dossiers and academic interpretations have different authority and scope. An English abstract does not support a reconstruction of an unread full text, and a regulator's hosting of an applicant dossier does not make it a religious ruling.

The paper register contains [95]{.claim data-claim="package_p09_source_records"} records: [63]{.claim data-claim="package_p09_sources_verified_with_note"} verified with a note by the recorded checking process, [22]{.claim data-claim="package_p09_sources_held"} held and [10]{.claim data-claim="package_p09_sources_not_retrieved"} not retrieved. All [95]{.claim data-claim="package_p09_human_verification_pending"} retain human verification pending. The phrase verified with a note must therefore be read as a bounded [source-audit]{.keep-word} status, not as independent scholarly approval.

:::

::: {data-package="P10"}

The shorter halal brief condenses that framework for a reader who needs the main conditions and common misreadings. Its contribution is explanatory rather than a separate body of consumer data. Its Pakistan [food-route]{.keep-word} statement reflects the earlier bounded search and must now be read alongside the Punjab instrument recovered for this edition.

The correction does not establish a [cultivated-specific]{.keep-word} approval. It establishes that a general [novel-food]{.keep-word} provision exists within the identified provincial instrument. Religious conditions, certification requirements and applicable food law still need to be matched to the product, jurisdiction and date. A later source can correct a documentary gap without resolving the whole [market-access]{.keep-word} question.

:::

::: {data-package="P11"}

The earlier [cultivated-chicken]{.keep-word} comparative package assembled country dossiers, [source-linked]{.keep-word} claims and a [scenario-model]{.keep-word} structure. Its numerical scenario output remains blocked by a missing compatible baseline. An empty result is retained as an empty result. The existence of a tested calculation routine does not authorize invented inputs or turn a documentary framework into a national adoption estimate.

The package helped separate evidence about permission, commercial readiness and demand. Those distinctions remain useful when reviewing a named product. A complete current dossier may settle a narrow institutional question even while broader scholarly disagreement continues. Conversely, a public announcement or conditional position cannot fill missing certification, authorization or consumer evidence.

:::

## Policy instruments have distinct legal and commercial meanings

The Food Safety and Standards Authority of India (FSSAI) administers the [food-regulatory]{.keep-word} instruments discussed here. India's record includes research support under Biotechnology for Economy, Environment and Employment (BioE3), [vegan-food]{.keep-word} rules, the [non-specified-food]{.keep-word} framework and dated labelling activity. The parliamentary update on supported [smart-protein]{.keep-word} projects is evidence of programme implementation, not evidence of food approval or repeat purchasing. The report keeps instrument dates, current document dates, proposed changes and future commencement dates separate.

Dates retain the source's event basis. The accompanying policy CSV separately records publication, application and commencement where established.

The [dairy-analogue]{.keep-word} labelling consultation is recorded as a consultation. Later amendments have their own stated commencement dates, so publication cannot be treated as immediate application. A filing associated with Biokraft is likewise distinct from its appearance on a later status list. A procedural stage or ambiguous list entry is not enlarged into a final permission.

Pakistan's record includes the Pakistan Halal Authority and relevant standards as well as the Punjab Food Authority instrument. The latter was notified before its later gazette publication and contains a [novel-food]{.keep-word} provision within a [functional-food]{.keep-word} and supplement framework. The source does not establish a [cultivated-specific]{.keep-word} authorization or prove that every cultivated product follows an identical route.

The correct comparison therefore concerns the documented functions of institutions and instruments. It cannot be reduced to India having policy and Pakistan having none. Nor can a count of policy rows establish regulatory effectiveness. The unresolved practical question is which documents a specified product would need and whether the responsible institutions have issued them.

## Cultural context describes variation without predicting demand

The culture matrix records dimensions such as dietary identity, religion and food practice, geography, household context and relevant institutions. Each entry retains its source and date. These observations help design questions and identify populations omitted by a proposed sample. They do not yield a national willingness score.

Vegetarian [self-identification]{.keep-word} differs from reported frequency of eating fish, chicken or meat. A respondent's definition can differ from a researcher's category, while frequency alone does not reveal quantities or motives. The report does not turn either measure into a market segment for a manufactured analogue. A person already avoiding meat has familiar alternatives that need to be included in the choice set.

Pakistan's urban share and halal institutions also describe different dimensions. Population geography does not measure exposure to alternative proteins, and an institutional mandate does not measure personal trust. Relevant [follow-up]{.keep-word} questions would record actual shopping access, information exposure, household [decision-making]{.keep-word} and [product-specific]{.keep-word} assurances rather than infer them from residence or affiliation.

The strongest interpretation supported here is that a uniform national consumer does not describe the evidence. Whether particular social or religious conditions independently affect purchase remains a question for a comparable study with specified products and prices.

## Review repairs change what the report can claim

::: {data-package="P04"}

The independent dashboard review identified problems in denominators, model construction, evidence tiers, policy interpretation, company scope, annotations and validation. The repair ledger records each finding and whether it was fixed, disclosed or declined, with an evidence path. The historical review is preserved so that a later passing check cannot erase the reason a calculation was changed.

The main numerical change is the withdrawal of Pakistan's complete intake estimate. The Indian benchmark now retains the [consumer-unit]{.keep-word} denominator and uses the EAR as the primary adult scenario. Derived outputs are identified as calculations rather than official observations. Company and policy entries retain dates, exclusions and documentary scope. Regional and nutrition displays keep incompatible measures apart.

The annotation work is confined to the limitations appendix. Collection stopped, [subject-country]{.keep-word} and topic eligibility were reviewed, and an independent [fresh-context]{.keep-word} pass was compared with the earlier labels. That comparison describes agreement between annotation runs. It is not accuracy, human adjudication, public opinion or evidence of consumer adoption.

Final browser evidence must correspond to the actual edition, and publication files require a reviewed allowlist. Rebuilds, source joins and export parity test implementation integrity. They cannot substitute for the source's research design or for a qualified reader's judgment about an interpretation. The report's remaining scientific limits survive a successful software check.

:::

Pakistan still lacks a compatible [complete-diet]{.keep-word} quantity and denominator basis in this model. The consumer evidence lacks a common bilateral instrument and observed adoption baseline. Current domestic sales, verified retail availability, operating capacities and [product-specific]{.keep-word} approvals remain unevenly documented. These are missing research inputs, not empty cells to be filled with a scenario midpoint.

The [public-source]{.keep-word} search has a defined stopping point. Failure to retrieve a source in that search means it was not established by this work, not that it cannot exist. Source ages remain visible, and access dates are not substituted for publication or fieldwork dates. A later researcher can revise a finding with new evidence while preserving this edition's record.

## The next data collection should answer a named question

The first task is to establish whether the identified [respondent-data]{.keep-word} routes contain reusable observations with compatible product and outcome definitions. Before analysis, each acquisition should record the file version, licence, sampling frame, language, recruitment, fieldwork dates, exclusions and exact variable meanings. A public repository title is insufficient evidence for any of these details.

A [product-availability]{.keep-word} study would separately define locations, retailers, observation dates and what counts as available. An [import-to-order]{.keep-word} listing should remain distinct from an item physically in stock. Recording package size and price per relevant serving would support an affordability comparison, while a stock record alone would not establish sales.

A small consumer pilot could then test the [meal-based]{.keep-word} comparison proposed in the brief. It should include people with different food expenditures, dietary practices and responsibilities for shopping or cooking. The immediate outcome would be whether the product description and choice task are understood. A purposive pilot would not estimate national demand.

Repeated purchasing and displacement require [follow-up]{.keep-word} observations. A diary or transaction study should record whether the new product replaced meat, dairy, pulses or another food, and whether the change persisted. Those outcomes would make the later forecast question more concrete. Until such evidence exists, the appropriate contribution is a traceable account of current conditions and explicit unanswered questions.

## Appendix C Review resolutions

Each finding retains its recorded state. These descriptions are concise; the [review CSV](../data/review_status.csv) preserves stable identifiers and the detailed repair account. Open [final-output]{.keep-word} requirements remain open until their evidence exists.

| Review finding and resolution |
| --- |
| [Shape checker accepts corrupted data]{#H1}. Recorded status: fixed. Recompute claims and test deliberate corruptions; historical shape checks remain dated. |
| [Pakistan animal-food protein uses basket-wide imputation]{#H2}. Recorded status: fixed. Withdraw the [pooled-basket]{.keep-word} total and use named physical conversions for partial foods. |
| [One Pakistan model value hides method sensitivity]{#H3}. Recorded status: fixed. Bind [partial-food]{.keep-word} range endpoints to named methods and coverage; withdraw the old [complete-total]{.keep-word} range. |
| [Independent second framing annotation absent]{#H4}. Recorded status: fixed. Compare a [fresh-context]{.keep-word} annotation with the earlier labels and disclose uncertainty and unknown earlier model identity. |
| [Publisher market mistaken for subject country]{#H5}. Recorded status: fixed. Keep publisher and subject geography separate; reconcile exclusions and holds; confine results to the report appendix. |
| [UnCrave closure absent]{#H6}. Recorded status: fixed. Retain the reported UnCrave closure as a dated event with an unknown exact cessation date. |
| [FSSAI plant-based rules absent]{#H7}. Recorded status: fixed. Add the [vegan-food]{.keep-word} rules and [dairy-analogue]{.keep-word} consultation with their distinct legal status. |
| [Adult RDA compared with all-age means]{#M8}. Recorded status: fixed. Use published [consumer-unit]{.keep-word} intake with a primary EAR scenario and an explicit denominator caveat. |
| [Pakistan rural/urban ranking caused by imputation]{#M9}. Recorded status: fixed. Remove rural and urban model rows and rankings from active projections while preserving historical artifacts. |
| [Official and experimental protein plotted as comparable]{#M10}. Recorded status: fixed. Place incompatible measures in separate panels and retain their source definitions. |
| [Unweighted group share resembles population prevalence]{#M11}. Recorded status: fixed. Remove the unweighted fraction of groups below the reference; it was not a population rate. |
| [Derived rows inherit input tiers; blanks remain]{#M12}. Recorded status: fixed. Tag analyst calculations as derived and retain [input-source]{.keep-word} tiers separately. |
| [Apply the revised freshness cutoff and integrate the saved parliamentary programme update]{#M13}. Recorded status: fixed. Apply the revised freshness cutoff and integrate the saved parliamentary programme update; retain the old rule as historical. |
| [Uneven food/feed inclusion; Pakistan coverage thin]{#M14}. Recorded status: fixed. Apply the [human-food]{.keep-word} versus feed rule consistently and retain explicit exclusions. |
| [Browser QA predates generated page]{#M15}. Recorded status: fixed. Require browser receipts for the actual edition bytes; old screenshots do not validate this page. |
| [Self-authored blocked status counts as success]{#M16}. Recorded status: fixed. Fail missing evidence and skipped required checks; [self-authored]{.keep-word} completion or blocked states cannot produce a pass. |
| [Broad Pakistan rule-absence claim is stale]{#M17}. Recorded status: fixed. Correct the broad Pakistan [rule-absence]{.keep-word} statement while distinguishing the general provincial provision from [cultivated-specific]{.keep-word} approval. |
| [Correct the revised HIES quantity denominator to per capita.]{#L1}. Recorded status: fixed. Correct the revised HIES quantity denominator to per capita. |
| [Name the Pakistan demographic and health survey by its actual fieldwork period.]{#L2}. Recorded status: fixed. Name the Pakistan demographic and health survey by its actual fieldwork period. |

## Appendix E Glossary

The key gives general categories; retained records can carry a more conservative historical grade. The survey extraction keeps B for sampled papers and C for the GFI commissioned market survey. These grades are preserved for provenance and do not establish a new quality review.

| Term | Meaning here |
| --- | --- |
| SOFI | The State of Food Security and Nutrition in the World: the interagency report series supplying the affordability estimates. |
| JME | Joint Child Malnutrition Estimates, produced by UNICEF, WHO and the World Bank. |
| MUIS | Majlis Ugama Islam Singapura, Singapore's Islamic Religious Council. |
| FSSAI | Food Safety and Standards Authority of India. |
| BioE3 | Biotechnology for Economy, Environment and Employment, India's biotechnology policy programme. |
| FBS | Food balance sheets: national accounting estimates of food available for human consumption. |
| HIES | Household Integrated Economic Survey: the Pakistani household survey and its stated quantity or expenditure measures. |
| HCES | Household Consumption Expenditure Survey: the Indian household survey used for published nutrient estimates. |
| MPCE and expenditure fractiles | Monthly [per-capita]{.keep-word} consumption expenditure. Fractiles rank the population from lower to higher expenditure, separately within rural and urban sectors; they do not measure protein deficiency. |
| NFHS | National Family Health Survey: respondent and household health measures, including reported food frequency. |
| EAR | Estimated Average Requirement: a requirement reference, not an observed individual intake. |
| RDA | Recommended Dietary Allowance: a distinct reference retained here as a labelled sensitivity. |
| Consumer unit | An [energy-based]{.keep-word} equivalence scale, not a [protein-equivalent]{.keep-word} person. |
| Tier | A source category whose use remains bounded by method, date, denominator and claim scope. |
| Tier A | National statistics offices, ministries, FAO/WHO/World Bank/OECD datasets, and [peer-reviewed]{.keep-word} papers with a stated sample. |
| Tier B | GFI, USDA FAS GAIN, UN agency reports and reputable trackers under the project classification. |
| Tier C | [Market-research]{.keep-word} summaries, news and company press releases; a recorded claim is not independent confirmation. |
| Tier D | Social media, Google Trends and LLM output. These cannot supply headline evidence; annotation results are confined to the report appendix. |
| Derived | An analyst calculation or model output; the source tier is retained separately. |
| Acceptance | A [study-specific]{.keep-word} response that must not be substituted for observed purchase or habitual use. |
| Adoption | An outcome requiring a stated product, population, behaviour, denominator and period. |
| Halal certification | A scoped institutional decision for a product and process, distinct from general religious conditions and food authorization. |

## Appendix F News framing remains annotation evidence

Collection stopped. The bounded review reconciles [194]{.claim data-claim="news_original_all_all"} original records to [28]{.claim data-claim="news_final_admissible_all_all"} admissible items, [164]{.claim data-claim="news_b_excluded_all_all"} excluded items and [2]{.claim data-claim="news_additional_held_all_all"} additional extent holds. The admissible set contains [27]{.claim data-claim="news_final_admissible_subject_country_india"} [India-focused]{.keep-word} items and [1]{.claim data-claim="news_final_admissible_subject_country_pakistan"} [Pakistan-focused]{.keep-word} item. That composition does not support a country comparison or an estimate of public sentiment.

The second annotation ran without the earlier labels. On the admissible primary set, the runs agree on [25]{.claim data-claim="news_primary_agreement_count"} of [28]{.claim data-claim="news_primary_n"} items. Cohen's kappa is [0.798561]{.claim data-claim="news_primary_kappa"}, with exact arithmetic [111]{.claim data-claim="news_primary_kappa_numerator"} divided by [139]{.claim data-claim="news_primary_kappa_denominator"}. The comparison is conditioned on the second run's eligibility decisions and the [source-access]{.keep-word} holds.

A sensitivity retaining the second run's held extents has [26]{.claim data-claim="news_provisional_sensitivity_agreement_count"} agreements among [30]{.claim data-claim="news_provisional_sensitivity_n"} items, with kappa [0.759036]{.claim data-claim="news_provisional_sensitivity_kappa"}. Its inclusion does not resolve the extent holds. The primary set includes [6]{.claim data-claim="news_final_admissible_uncertainty_b_high"} [high-uncertainty]{.keep-word} [second-run]{.keep-word} decisions. Neither statistic measures accuracy or human consensus.

```text
Second-run runtime model: gpt-6-astra
Earlier-run runtime model: not established
Human adjudication: pending
```

The method record preserves the prompt, immutable inputs, individual labels, exclusions and disagreements locally. Runtime identity does not establish independent model families, and the unknown earlier identity is not filled by inference. Only bounded metadata and derived results are prepared for the report; article bodies are excluded from publication. No framing statistic appears in the edition's content or downloads.
